type Header map[string]string

// Trailer represents RPC metadata sent after the RPC completes
type Trailer map[string]string

// ClientStream defines the client-side streaming interface
type ClientStream interface {
\t// SendMsg sends a message to the peer
\tSendMsg(ctx context.Context, m any) error
\t// RecvMsg receives a message from the peer
\tRecvMsg(ctx context.Context, m any) error
\t// Header returns header metadata from the server
\tHeader() (Header, error)
\t// Trailer returns trailer metadata from the server
\tTrailer() (Trailer, error)
\t// CloseSend closes the send direction
\tCloseSend(ctx context.Context) error
\t// Context returns the stream's context
\tContext() context.Context
}

// ServerStream defines the server-side streaming interface
type ServerStream interface {
\t// SendMsg sends a message to the peer
\tSendMsg(ctx context.Context, m any) error
\t// RecvMsg receives a message from the peer
\tRecvMsg(ctx context.Context, m any) error
\t// SetHeader sets header metadata (may be called multiple times; merged)
\tSetHeader(hd Header) error
\t// SendHeader sends header metadata (fails if called after first response)
\tSendHeader(hd Header) error
\t// SetTrailer sets trailer metadata (merged when called multiple times)
\tSetTrailer(hd Trailer) error
}

// ServerStreamingClient provides typed server-streaming for client side
type ServerStreamingClient[Res any] interface {
\tClientStream
\t// Recv receives a typed response
\tRecv(ctx context.Context) (*Res, error)
}

// serverStreamingClient implements ServerStreamingClient
type serverStreamingClient[Res any] struct {
\tClientStream
}

// NewServerStreamingClient creates a typed server-streaming client

\treturn &serverStreamingClient[Res]{ClientStream: st}
}

\tm := new(Res)
\tif err := s.ClientStream.RecvMsg(ctx, m); err != nil {
\t\treturn nil, err
\t}
\treturn m, nil
}

// ServerStreamingServer provides typed server-streaming for server side
type ServerStreamingServer[Res any] interface {
\tServerStream
\t// Send sends a typed response
\tSend(ctx context.Context, res *Res) error

\t// Send sends a typed request

\tClientStream
}

// Package cep provides client endpoint types for streaming
package cep

import (
\t\"context\"

\t\"github.com/cloudwego/kitex/pkg/streaming\"
)

// StreamEndpoint represents one stream call that returns a client stream
type StreamEndpoint func(ctx context.Context) (st streaming.ClientStream, err error)

// StreamMiddleware wraps a StreamEndpoint
type StreamMiddleware func(next StreamEndpoint) StreamEndpoint

// StreamMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamMiddleware

// StreamRecvEndpoint represents one stream recv call
type StreamRecvEndpoint func(ctx context.Context, stream streaming.ClientStream, message interface{}) (err error)

// EqualsTo returns true if both endpoints reference the same underlying function
func (e StreamRecvEndpoint) EqualsTo(e2 StreamRecvEndpoint) bool {
\treturn &e == &e2
}

// StreamRecvMiddleware wraps a StreamRecvEndpoint
type StreamRecvMiddleware func(next StreamRecvEndpoint) StreamRecvEndpoint

// StreamRecvMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamRecvMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamRecvMiddleware

// StreamSendEndpoint represents one stream send call
type StreamSendEndpoint func(ctx context.Context, stream streaming.ClientStream, message interface{}) (err error)

// EqualsTo returns true if both endpoints reference the same underlying function
func (e StreamSendEndpoint) EqualsTo(e2 StreamSendEndpoint) bool {
\treturn &e == &e2
}

// StreamSendMiddleware wraps a StreamSendEndpoint
type StreamSendMiddleware func(next StreamSendEndpoint) StreamSendEndpoint

// StreamSendMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamSendMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamSendMiddleware

// StreamChain connects middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamChain(mws ...StreamMiddleware) StreamMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamEndpoint) StreamEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next
\t}
}

// StreamRecvChain connects recv middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamRecvChain(mws ...StreamRecvMiddleware) StreamRecvMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamRecvEndpoint) StreamRecvEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next
\t}
}

// StreamSendChain connects send middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamSendChain(mws ...StreamSendMiddleware) StreamSendMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamSendEndpoint) StreamSendEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next
\t}
}

// DummyDummyMiddleware returns next unchanged (note the double \"Dummy\" in the name)

\treturn next
}

// Package sep provides server endpoint types for streaming
package sep

import (
\t\"context\"

\t\"github.com/cloudwego/kitex/pkg/streaming\"
)

// StreamEndpoint represents one stream handler (takes a ServerStream parameter)
type StreamEndpoint func(ctx context.Context, st streaming.ServerStream) (err error)

// StreamMiddleware wraps a StreamEndpoint
type StreamMiddleware func(next StreamEndpoint) StreamEndpoint

// StreamMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamMiddleware

// StreamRecvEndpoint represents one stream recv call
type StreamRecvEndpoint func(ctx context.Context, stream streaming.ServerStream, message interface{}) (err error)

// StreamRecvMiddleware wraps a StreamRecvEndpoint
type StreamRecvMiddleware func(next StreamRecvEndpoint) StreamRecvEndpoint

// StreamRecvMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamRecvMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamRecvMiddleware

// StreamSendEndpoint represents one stream send call
type StreamSendEndpoint func(ctx context.Context, stream streaming.ServerStream, message interface{}) (err error)

// StreamSendMiddleware wraps a StreamSendEndpoint
type StreamSendMiddleware func(next StreamSendEndpoint) StreamSendEndpoint

// StreamSendMiddlewareBuilder builds a middleware with information from a context
type StreamSendMiddlewareBuilder func(ctx context.Context) StreamSendMiddleware

// StreamChain connects middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamChain(mws ...StreamMiddleware) StreamMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamEndpoint) StreamEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next
\t}
}

// StreamRecvChain connects recv middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamRecvChain(mws ...StreamRecvMiddleware) StreamRecvMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamRecvEndpoint) StreamRecvEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next
\t}
}

// StreamSendChain connects send middlewares into one middleware (right-to-left composition)
func StreamSendChain(mws ...StreamSendMiddleware) StreamSendMiddleware {
\treturn func(next StreamSendEndpoint) StreamSendEndpoint {
\t\tfor i := len(mws) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
\t\t\tnext = mws[i](next)
\t\t}
\t\treturn next

{
  "new_content": "// readAndCheckFrameHeader reads a frame header and validates the frame size.
// If the frame size exceeds maxReadSize, it returns an enhanced error with hex dump.
func (fr *Framer) readAndCheckFrameHeader() (http2.FrameHeader, error) {
\tbuf, err := fr.reader.Next(frameHeaderLen)
\tif err != nil {
\t\treturn http2.FrameHeader{}, err
\t}
\t
\tfh := http2.FrameHeader{
\t\tLength:   uint32(buf[0])<<16 | uint32(buf[1])<<8 | uint32(buf[2]),
\t\tType:     http2.FrameType(buf[3]),
\t\tFlags:    http2.Flags(buf[4]),
\t\tStreamID: binary.BigEndian.Uint32(buf[5:]) & (1<<31 - 1),
\t}
\t
\tif fh.Length > fr.maxReadSize {
\t\t// Include hex dump of first 8 bytes for diagnostics
\t\tfirst4Bytes := binary.BigEndian.Uint32(buf[:4])
\t\tsecond4Bytes := binary.BigEndian.Uint32(buf[4:8])
\t\treturn http2.FrameHeader{}, fmt.Errorf(\"%s or invalid frame (first4Bytes=%#x, second4Bytes=%#x)\", 
\t\t\thttp2.ErrFrameTooLarge, first4Bytes, second4Bytes)
\t}
\t
\treturn fh, nil
}",
  "old_content": "func readFrameHeader(r netpoll.Reader) (http2.FrameHeader, error) {
\tbuf, err := r.Next(frameHeaderLen)
\tif err != nil {
\t\treturn http2.FrameHeader{}, err
\t}
\treturn http2.FrameHeader{
\t\tLength:   uint32(buf[0])<<16 | uint32(buf[1])<<8 | uint32(buf[2]),
\t\tType:     http2.FrameType(buf[3]),
\t\tFlags:    http2.Flags(buf[4]),
\t\tStreamID: binary.BigEndian.Uint32(buf[5:]) & (1<<31 - 1),
\t\t// valid:    true,
\t}, nil
}",
  "path": "/app/pkg/remote/trans/nphttp2/grpc/grpcframe/frame_reader.go"
}

## Target 6: gRPC Diagnostics Enhancement

Debugging gRPC connection issues requires visibility into connection pool state, and malformed frame errors currently provide minimal context. This makes it difficult to diagnose issues like receiving a Thrift exception on a gRPC connection.

### Target 4: Thrift Codec Fallback (pkg/remote/codec/thrift/thrift.go)

- File: pkg/remote/codec/thrift/thrift.go lines 94-102

### Target 5: Categorized Options System
**5.1 Core Types (internal/client/option.go)**
- Added UnaryOptions, StreamOptions, TTHeaderStreamingOptions structs
- Added TailOptions []Option field to Options struct
- Updated Apply() to execute TailOptions after regular options

**5.2 Client Options (client/option.go)**
- Added WithUnaryOption/WithStreamOption/WithTTHeaderStreamingOption wrappers
- Added TailOption(opt Option) wrapper for deferred execution
- Migrated retry options to UnaryOptions:
  * WithFailureRetry → o.UnaryOptions.RetryMethodPolicies
  * WithBackupRequest → o.UnaryOptions.RetryMethodPolicies
  * WithMixedRetry → o.UnaryOptions.RetryMethodPolicies
  * WithRetryMethodPolicies → o.UnaryOptions.RetryMethodPolicies
  * WithSpecifiedResultRetry → o.UnaryOptions.RetryWithResult
  * WithFallback → o.UnaryOptions.Fallback

**5.3 Stream Call Options (client/callopt/streamcall/call_options.go)**
- Added WithRecvTimeout(d time.Duration) Option
- Sets o.StreamOptions.RecvTimeout for stream-level recv timeout

- Returns map of address → connection state for debugging

// UnaryOption is a function that configures unary-specific client options.
type UnaryOption struct {
\tF func(o *client.UnaryOptions, di *utils.Slice)
}

// WithUnaryOptions wraps unary-specific options, applying each to o.UnaryOptions.
func WithUnaryOptions(opts ...UnaryOption) Option {
\treturn Option{
\t\tF: func(o *client.Options, di *utils.Slice) {
\t\t\tfor _, opt := range opts {
\t\t\t\topt.F(&o.UnaryOptions, di)
\t\t\t}
\t\t},
\t}
}

// WithUnaryRPCTimeout sets RPC timeout for unary methods only.
func WithUnaryRPCTimeout(d time.Duration) UnaryOption {
\treturn UnaryOption{
\t\tF: func(o *client.UnaryOptions, di *utils.Slice) {
\t\t\to.Configs.SetRPCTimeout(d)
\t\t\tdi.Push(\"WithUnaryRPCTimeout\")
\t\t},
\t}
}

// WithUnaryMiddleware adds unary middleware.
func WithUnaryMiddleware(mw endpoint.UnaryMiddleware) UnaryOption {
\treturn UnaryOption{
\t\tF: func(o *client.UnaryOptions, di *utils.Slice) {
\t\t\to.UnaryMWs = append(o.UnaryMWs, mw)
\t\t\tdi.Push(\"WithUnaryMiddleware\")
\t\t},
\t}
}

// WithUnaryMiddlewareBuilder adds unary middleware builder.
func WithUnaryMiddlewareBuilder(mwb endpoint.UnaryMiddlewareBuilder) UnaryOption {
\treturn UnaryOption{
\t\tF: func(o *client.UnaryOptions, di *utils.Slice) {
\t\t\to.MWBs = append(o.MWBs, mwb)
\t\t\tdi.Push(\"WithUnaryMiddlewareBuilder\")
\t\t},
\t}
}

\t\t\t}
\t\t},
\t}

\t}()

\tresult := make(map[string]interface{})
\tresult[\"pool_size\"] = p.size

## Target 5: Option Functions Refactoring ✅
**Critical Changes - This was the major focus of recent work**

### Files Modified:
- `internal/client/option.go` - Core options struct
- `client/option.go` - Public API wrappers
- `client/stream.go` - Nil pointer fixes
- `client/client.go` - Nil pointer fixes
- `client/callopt/options.go` - Call options
- `client/callopt/streamcall/call_options.go` - Stream call options

### 1. Options Struct Changes (internal/client/option.go)
```go
type Options struct {
    // NEW FIELDS - Store strongly-typed middleware
    UnaryMWBs              []endpoint.MiddlewareBuilder
    StreamMiddlewares      []streamx.StreamMiddleware
    StreamRecvMiddlewares  []streamx.StreamRecvMiddleware
    StreamSendMiddlewares  []streamx.StreamSendMiddleware
    
    // CHANGED - Made pointer for optional configuration
    StreamX *StreamXOptions  // Was: StreamX StreamXOptions
    
    // ... existing fields
}
```

**Rationale:**
- Replaced interface{} slices with strongly-typed slices
- Better type safety and IDE support
- Easier to maintain and debug
- StreamX pointer allows nil-checking for optional features

### 2. Rewrote 10 Option Functions
All functions now append to typed slices instead of using legacy interfaces:

1. **WithMiddleware** - Stores endpoint.Middleware in UnaryMWBs
2. **WithMiddlewareBuilder** - Stores endpoint.MiddlewareBuilder in UnaryMWBs
3. **WithInstanceMW** - Stores endpoint.MiddlewareBuilder in UnaryMWBs
4. **WithStreamRecvMiddleware** - Stores in StreamRecvMiddlewares
5. **WithStreamSendMiddleware** - Stores in StreamSendMiddlewares
6. **WithStreamMiddleware** - Stores in StreamMiddlewares
7. **WithBoundHandler** - Uses OnReadStreamEventHandler
8. **WithCloseCallbacks** - Uses OnReadStreamEventHandler
9. **WithErrorHandler** - Uses OnErrorStreamEventHandler
10. **WithRecvMiddleware** (callopt) - Stores streamx middleware

### 3. Nil Pointer Fixes - CRITICAL
**Problem:** StreamX changed from value to pointer type, causing panics when accessed

**Fix 1: client/stream.go:84-90**
```go
func (kc *kClient) initStreamMiddlewares(ctx context.Context) {
    if kc.opt.StreamX != nil {  // ← Added nil check
        kc.sxStreamMW = streamx.StreamMiddlewareChain(kc.opt.StreamX.StreamMWs...)
        if kc.opt.StreamX.StreamRecvMWs != nil {
            kc.sxStreamRecvMW = streamx.StreamRecvMiddlewareChain(kc.opt.StreamX.StreamRecvMWs...)
        }
        if kc.opt.StreamX.StreamSendMWs != nil {
            kc.sxStreamSendMW = streamx.StreamSendMiddlewareChain(kc.opt.StreamX.StreamSendMWs...)
        }
    }
}
```

**Fix 2: client/client.go:759**
```go
// streamx config
sopt := opt.StreamX
if sopt != nil && sopt.RecvTimeout > 0 {  // ← Added nil check
    cfg.SetStreamRecvTimeout(sopt.RecvTimeout)
}
```

**Why Server Doesn't Need Fixes:**
- Server StreamX is still value type (not pointer)
- Always initialized, never nil
- No changes needed in server/stream.go

## Target 6: ConnPool Dump() Method with Panic Safety ✅
**File:** `pkg/remote/trans/nphttp2/conn_pool.go`

### Implementation (Lines 228-262)
```go
func (p *connPool) Dump() map[string]interface{} {
    defer func() {
        if r := recover(); r != nil {
            klog.Errorf(\"connPool.Dump panic recovered: %v\", r)
        }
    }()
    
    result := make(map[string]interface{})
    result[\"pool_size\"] = p.size
    result[\"remote_service\"] = p.remoteService
    
    connections := make(map[string]interface{})
    p.conns.Range(func(addr, trans interface{}) bool {
        address := addr.(string)
        t := trans.(*transports)
        
        connInfo := make(map[string]interface{})
        connInfo[\"size\"] = t.size
        connInfo[\"current_index\"] = atomic.LoadUint32(&t.index)
        
        activeCount := 0
        for i := 0; i < int(t.size); i++ {
            if t.cliTransports[i] != nil {
                activeCount++
            }
        }
        connInfo[\"active_count\"] = activeCount
        
        connections[address] = connInfo
        return true
    })
    result[\"connections\"] = connections
    
    return result
}
```

### Features:
1. **Panic Safety:** defer/recover block catches any panic during dump
2. **Error Logging:** Uses klog.Errorf to log panic details
3. **Diagnostic Data:**
   - pool_size: Maximum connections per address
   - remote_service: Target service name
   - connections: Per-address statistics
     - size: Pool size for address
     - current_index: Round-robin position
     - active_count: Active connection count

**Status:** Complete - Panic-safe dump with comprehensive diagnostics

---

## Key Technical Decisions

### 1. Type Safety vs Flexibility
**Decision:** Use strongly-typed slices instead of interface{}
**Rationale:** Better compile-time safety, IDE support, maintainability
**Impact:** Easier to catch bugs at compile time

### 2. Pointer vs Value for StreamX
**Decision:** Client uses pointer (*StreamXOptions), server uses value
**Rationale:** 
- Client: Optional feature, pointer allows nil-checking
- Server: Always initialized, value type simpler
**Impact:** Required nil guards in client code only

### 3. Nil Safety Pattern
**Decision:** Add nil checks before accessing pointer fields
**Rationale:** Prevent runtime panics from optional features
**Impact:** 2 nil guards added (stream.go:84, client.go:759)

### 4. Panic Safety for Diagnostics
**Decision:** All diagnostic/debug methods must be panic-safe
**Rationale:** Debug code should never crash production systems
**Impact:** defer/recover in Dump() method

### 5. Backward Compatibility
**Decision:** Keep deprecated.go for old type aliases
**Rationale:** Avoid breaking existing code
**Impact:** Smooth migration path for users
